<script>
// 모바일 슬라이드 메뉴 - 사업소개 토글 활성화 스크립트
(function() {
    'use strict';
    
    function initMenuToggle() {
        // 사업소개 메뉴 아이템 찾기
        const businessMenuItem = document.querySelector('li[data-code="m20250808b76bbf068252f"]');
        if (!businessMenuItem) return;
        
        // 필요한 요소들 찾기
        const plainText = businessMenuItem.querySelector('.plain_name');
        const submenu = businessMenuItem.querySelector('ul[data-index="7"]') || 
                       businessMenuItem.querySelector('ul.collapse') ||
                       businessMenuItem.querySelector('ul');
        
        if (!plainText || !submenu) return;
        
        // plain_name에 토글 클래스 추가하여 토글 버튼으로 만들기
        plainText.classList.add('_toggle_btn', 'toggle-btn');
        
        // 시각적 스타일 보존
        plainText.style.cursor = 'pointer';
        plainText.style.display = 'inline';
        plainText.style.fontSize = 'inherit';
        plainText.style.fontWeight = 'inherit';
        plainText.style.color = 'inherit';
    }
    
    // 실행 함수
    function executeWhenReady() {
        // 여러 시점에서 시도
        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
                console.log('📄 DOM 로드 완료');
                setTimeout(initMenuToggle, 500);
            });
        } else {
            console.log('📄 이미 DOM 로드됨');
            setTimeout(initMenuToggle, 500);
        }
        
        // 추가적으로 윈도우 로드 후에도 시도
        window.addEventListener('load', function() {
            console.log('🌐 윈도우 로드 완료');
            setTimeout(initMenuToggle, 1000);
        });
    }
    
    executeWhenReady();
    
})();

</script>

/**사이트 모드**/
:root { --extra-padding: 0px; }
@media (max-width: 991px){
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile #doz_header_wrap + .section_first.mobile_section_first.visual_section,
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile #doz_header_wrap + .mobile_section_first.visual_section,
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile > .section_first.pc_section.visual_section,
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile > .mobile_section_first.visual_section,
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile > .section_first.pc_section.scroll-to-fixed-fixed,
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile > .mobile_section_first.mobile_section.scroll-to-fixed-fixed {
		padding-top: 0 !important;
	}
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile > .section_first.mobile_section_first,
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile > section > .section_first.mobile_section_first:not(.visual_section),
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile > .mobile_section_first,
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile.section_fixed_disable > .section_first.pc_section.scroll-to-fixed-fixed,
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile #doz_header_wrap + .section_first.mobile_section_first.visual_section .op,
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile #doz_header_wrap + .mobile_section_first.visual_section .op,
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile #doz_header_wrap + .section_first.pc_section.visual_section .op,
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile #doz_header_wrap ~ .section_first.pc_section.visual_section .op,
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile > .mobile_section_first.visual_section .op,
	.doz_sys.new_header_overlay_mobile.section_fixed_disable > .mobile_section_first.mobile_section.scroll-to-fixed-fixed {
		padding-top: calc(48px + var(--extra-padding))  !important;
	}

}

	top: 48px; z-index: 100;

padding-top: 0 !important;

		background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0);

	}
	#w20250808e011de02c0fcc .widget.image:hover .hover_overlay {
		background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0);
		z-index: 6;
		opacity: 1;
	}

/* 둥둥 떠다니는 애니메이션 효과 */
#w20250808e011de02c0fcc {
    animation: floating 3s ease-in-out infinite;
    transform-origin: center center;
}

/* 키프레임 정의 - 부드러운 상하 움직임 */
@keyframes floating {
    0%, 100% {
        transform: translateY(0px) rotate(0deg);
    }
    25% {
        transform: translateY(-10px) rotate(1deg);
    }
    50% {
        transform: translateY(-15px) rotate(0deg);
    }
    75% {
        transform: translateY(-5px) rotate(-1deg);
    }
}



/* 추가 옵션: 좌우로도 살짝 움직이게 하려면 아래 코드 사용 */

    }
    75% {
        transform: translateY(-5px) translateX(-3px);
    }
}

/* 초기 상태: 요소들 숨김 */
.tp-primary-headline,
.tp-vertical-divider {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
    will-change: opacity, transform;
}

/* 세로선 초기 상태 */
.tp-vertical-divider {
    height: 0;
    transform: translateY(0);
    opacity: 0;
}

/* 스크롤 진입 시 애니메이션 */
.tp-animate .tp-primary-headline {
    animation: fadeInUp 1.2s ease-out 0.3s both;
}

.tp-animate .tp-vertical-divider {
    animation: lineGrowDown 1.5s ease-out 0.8s both;
}

/* 애니메이션 키프레임 */
@keyframes fadeInUp {
    from {
        opacity: 0;
        transform: translateY(30px);
    }
    to {
        opacity: 1;
        transform: translateY(0);
    }
}

@keyframes lineGrowDown {
    from {
        opacity: 0;
        height: 0;
        transform: scaleY(0);
        transform-origin: top;
    }
    50% {
        opacity: 1;
        height: 160px;
        transform: scaleY(0.5);
    }
    to {
        opacity: 1;
        height: 160px;
        transform: scaleY(1);
    }
}

/* 데스크톱 스타일 */
@media (min-width: 1200px) {
    .tp-hero-heading-wrapper {
        padding: 80px 20px;
    }
    
    .tp-primary-headline {
        font-size: 72px;
        letter-spacing: -2.5px;
    }
    
    .tp-vertical-divider {
        margin: 60px auto;
    }
}

/* 태블릿 스타일 */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
    .tp-hero-heading-wrapper {
        padding: 50px 15px;
    }
    
    .tp-primary-headline {
        font-size: 48px;
        line-height: 1.4;
        letter-spacing: -1.5px;
    }
    
    .tp-vertical-divider {
        margin: 40px auto;
    }
    
    @keyframes lineGrowDown {
        from {
            opacity: 0;
            height: 0;
            transform: scaleY(0);
        }
        50% {
            opacity: 1;
            height: 120px;
            transform: scaleY(0.5);
        }
        to {
            opacity: 1;
            height: 120px;
            transform: scaleY(1);
        }
    }
}

/* 모바일 스타일 */
@media (max-width: 480px) {
    .tp-hero-heading-wrapper {
        padding: 40px 15px;
    }
    
    .tp-primary-headline {
        font-size: 36px;
        line-height: 1.5;
        letter-spacing: -1px;
    }
    
    .tp-vertical-divider {
        width: 2px;
        margin: 30px auto;
    }
    
    @keyframes lineGrowDown {
        from {
            opacity: 0;
            height: 0;
            transform: scaleY(0);
        }
        50% {
            opacity: 1;
            height: 100px;
            transform: scaleY(0.5);
        }
        to {
            opacity: 1;
            height: 100px;
            transform: scaleY(1);
        }
    }
}

/* 초소형 모바일 */
@media (max-width: 360px) {
    .tp-primary-headline {
        font-size: 32px;
    }
    
    @keyframes lineGrowDown {
        from {
            opacity: 0;
            height: 0;
            transform: scaleY(0);
        }
        50% {
            opacity: 1;
            height: 80px;
            transform: scaleY(0.5);
        }
        to {
            opacity: 1;
            height: 80px;
            transform: scaleY(1);
        }
    }
}

/* 위젯 w20250730fc25d58d06450 모바일 폰트 크기 조정 */

/* 태블릿 (768px 이하) */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
    #w20250730fc25d58d06450,
    #w20250730fc25d58d06450 * {
        font-size: 36px !important;
    }
    
    /* 제목이나 메인 텍스트가 있다면 */
    #w20250730fc25d58d06450 h1,
    #w20250730fc25d58d06450 h2,

    #w20250730fc25d58d06450 h4,
    #w20250730fc25d58d06450 h5,
    #w20250730fc25d58d06450 h6 {
        font-size: 36px !important;
    }
    
    /* 문단이나 일반 텍스트 */
    #w20250730fc25d58d06450 p,
    #w20250730fc25d58d06450 span,
    #w20250730fc25d58d06450 div {
        font-size: 36px !important;
    }
    
    /* 링크나 버튼 텍스트 */
    #w20250730fc25d58d06450 a,
    #w20250730fc25d58d06450 button {
        font-size: 36px !important;
    }
}

/* 초기 상태: 요소들 숨김 */
.talkple-solution-title,
.talkple-info-card-left,
.talkple-info-card-right {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(30px);
    will-change: opacity, transform;
}

/* 더 빠른 부드러운 깜빡이는 효과 - 구조 텍스트 */
.talkple-typing-highlight {
    animation: smoothBlink 0.8s infinite ease-in-out;
    display: inline-block;
}

@keyframes smoothBlink {
    0%, 30% {
        opacity: 1;
    }
    40%, 80% {
        opacity: 0;
    }
    90%, 100% {
        opacity: 1;
    }
}

/* 스크롤 진입 시 빠른 순차 애니메이션 */
.talkple-animate .talkple-solution-title {
    animation: fadeInUp 1s ease-out 0.2s both;
}

.talkple-animate .talkple-info-card-left {
    animation: fadeInUp 0.8s ease-out 0.8s both;
}

.talkple-animate .talkple-info-card-right {
    animation: fadeInUp 0.8s ease-out 1s both;
}

/* 애니메이션 키프레임 */
@keyframes fadeInUp {
    from {
        opacity: 0;
        transform: translateY(30px);
    }
    to {
        opacity: 1;
        transform: translateY(0);
    }
}

/* 카드 부드러운 호버 효과 - 위로 올라가고 보라색 그림자 */
.talkple-info-card-left,
.talkple-info-card-right {
    cursor: pointer !important;
    transition: all 0.3s ease !important;
}

.talkple-info-card-left:hover,
.talkple-info-card-right:hover {
    transform: translateY(-8px) !important;
    box-shadow: 0 20px 40px rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.3), 0 10px 20px rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.15) !important;
}

/* 데스크톱 스타일 */
@media (min-width: 1200px) {
    .talkple-main-header-section {
        padding: 60px 20px 30px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-content-showcase {
        padding: 50px 20px 100px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-solution-title {
        font-size: 72px !important;
        letter-spacing: -2.5px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-cards-container {
        gap: 40px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-info-card-left,
    .talkple-info-card-right {
        padding: 50px 40px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-info-card-left:hover,
    .talkple-info-card-right:hover {
        transform: translateY(-10px) !important;
        box-shadow: 0 25px 50px rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.4), 0 15px 25px rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.2) !important;
    }
}

/* 태블릿 스타일 */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
    .talkple-main-header-section {
        padding: 40px 15px 15px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-content-showcase {
        padding: 30px 15px 60px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-solution-title {
        font-size: 48px !important;
        line-height: 1.4 !important;
        letter-spacing: -1.5px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-cards-container {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr !important;
        gap: 25px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-info-card-left,
    .talkple-info-card-right {
        padding: 35px 25px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-card-title-first,
    .talkple-card-title-second {
        font-size: 28px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-card-title-first span,
    .talkple-card-title-second span {
        font-size: 22px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-info-card-left:hover,
    .talkple-info-card-right:hover {
        transform: translateY(-6px) !important;
        box-shadow: 0 18px 35px rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.25), 0 8px 15px rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.15) !important;
    }
}

/* 모바일 스타일 */
@media (max-width: 480px) {
    .talkple-main-header-section {
        padding: 30px 15px 10px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-content-showcase {
        padding: 20px 15px 50px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-solution-title {
        font-size: 36px !important;
        line-height: 1.5 !important;
        letter-spacing: -1px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-solution-title span {
        font-size: 32px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-cards-container {
        gap: 20px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-info-card-left,
    .talkple-info-card-right {
        padding: 30px 20px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-card-title-first,
    .talkple-card-title-second {
        font-size: 24px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-card-title-first span,
    .talkple-card-title-second span {
        font-size: 20px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-info-card-left:hover,
    .talkple-info-card-right:hover {
        transform: translateY(-4px) !important;
        box-shadow: 0 15px 30px rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.2), 0 8px 15px rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.1) !important;
    }
}

/* 초소형 모바일 */
@media (max-width: 360px) {
    .talkple-solution-title {
        font-size: 32px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-solution-title span {
        font-size: 28px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-info-card-left,
    .talkple-info-card-right {
        padding: 25px 15px !important;
    }
    
    .talkple-info-card-left:hover,
    .talkple-info-card-right:hover {
        transform: translateY(-3px) !important;
        box-shadow: 0 12px 25px rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.15) !important;
    }
}

	.footer-section .nav.nav-stacked.footer-all-nav li {
		float:left;
	}
	.footer-section .nav.nav-stacked.footer-all-nav li a {
		padding   :3px 15px;
		font-size        :14px;
	}
	.footer-section .nav.nav-stacked.footer-all-nav li a:first-child {
		padding-left:0;
	}
	.footer-section .nav.nav-stacked.footer-all-nav li a:hover {
		background:none;
		opacity: 0.7;
	}
	.nav-stacked > li + li {
		margin-top:0;
	}
	.footer-section .foot-sociallink .btn-group {
		display: block;
	}
	.footer-section .foot-sociallink .btn-group {
		margin-top: 14px;
	}
	.footer-section .foot-sociallink .btn-group .btn {
		background: none;
		font-size:20px;
		border-radius: 0;
		border:none;
		padding: 0px;
		padding-right: 15px;
		padding-left: 0px;
		border-color: transparent;
		margin-left: 0px;
	}
	.footer-section .foot-sociallink .btn-group .btn:last-child {
		padding-right: 0px;
	}
	.footer-section .foot-sociallink .btn-group .btn i {
		color:rgba(255,255,255,1) ;
	}
	.footer-section .foot-sociallink .btn-group .btn i.icon_naver {
		font-family: "Arial Black", "AvenirNext-Heavy";
		transform: translateY(-1px);
		font-style: normal;
	}
	.footer-section .foot-sociallink .btn-group .btn:hover {
		opacity: 0.7;
		color:inherit;
	}
	#doz_footer .footer-section .foot-app-menu {
		margin-top: 30px;
		overflow: hidden;
	}
	#doz_footer .footer-section .foot-app-menu > div {
		float:left;
		margin: 2.5px;
	}
	#doz_footer .footer-section .foot-app-menu .btn {
		width: 195px;
		height: 60px;
		padding: 0 24px;
		color:#fff
	 }
	#doz_footer .footer-section .foot-app-menu .sm-txt {
		font-size        :14px;
		color: #999;
		letter-spacing: 0;
	}
	#doz_footer .footer-section .foot-app-menu .lg-txt {
		font-size        :14px;
		font-weight: bold;
		letter-spacing: 0;
		color: #fff;
	}
	@media (max-width:992px) {
		.footer-section {
			font-size : 14px;
		}

		.footer-section .footer-wrap {
			padding : 0;
		}

		.footer-section .foot-sociallink {
			position : inherit;
			order    : 4;
			overflow: hidden;
		}
	}
	@media (max-width:640px) {
		#doz_footer .footer-section .foot-app-menu {
			margin-top: 20px;
		}
		#doz_footer .footer-section .foot-app-menu .btn {
			width: 100%;
			letter-spacing: 0;
		}
		#doz_footer .footer-section .foot-app-menu > div {
			float: none;
			margin: 0;
			display: table-cell;
			padding: 0 2.5px 0 0;
		}
		#doz_footer .footer-section .foot-app-menu > div.apple {
			padding: 0 0 0 2.5px;
		}
		#doz_footer .footer-section .foot-app-menu .lg-txt {
			font-size:14px;
		}
	}
	@media all and (min-width:320px) and (max-width:639px) {
		#doz_footer .footer-section .foot-app-menu .btn {
			padding: 0 8px;
		}
	}
	#doz_footer .footer-section .footer-company-info__item {
        display: inline-block;
        margin-right: 8px;
    }
    #doz_footer .footer-section .footer-company-info #footer-company-info-line-1 {
        margin-bottom: 6px;
    }
    #doz_footer .footer-section .footer-company-info #footer-company-info-line-2 {
        margin-bottom: 20px;
    }

<script>
(function() {
	function getCookie(name) {
		const value = "; " + document.cookie;
		const parts = value.split("; " + name + "=");
		if (parts.length === 2) return parts.pop().split(";").shift();
		return null;
	}

	window.addEventListener('pageshow', function(event) {
		const cookieLoginState = getCookie('_imweb_login_state');
		const pageIsGuest = window.IS_GUEST;

		// 쿠키: Y(로그인), 페이지: true(게스트) → 불일치
		// 쿠키: N(비로그인), 페이지: false(로그인) → 불일치
		if (cookieLoginState !== null) {
			const cookieIsGuest = (cookieLoginState === 'N');
			if (pageIsGuest !== cookieIsGuest) {
				location.reload();
			}
		}
	});
})();
</script>
<style> 
/* PC 기본으로 <br>을 숨김 */ 
.mobile-only { display: none; } 

/* 모바일에서는 숨기고 PC에서만 보이게 */ 
.pc-only { display: inline; } 
/* 화면 너비가 768px 이하일 때만 <br>을 보이게 함 */ 
@media (max-width: 768px) { 
.mobile-only { display: inline; } 
.pc-only { display: none; 
} 
}

<script>
	// 담기 완료 시 헤더 장바구니 뱃지 갱신 (IMIO-7835) — 뱃지 DOM 은 호스트 소유라 위젯 이벤트를
	// 셸이 수신해 처리한다. 레거시 담기(site_shop.js addCart)와 동일 규칙: 뱃지는 "상품 종류 수"라
	// 같은 상품 재담기(prodFound)는 증가하지 않고, 0→1 이 될 때 disabled 해제로 뱃지를 표시한다.
	(function () {
		if (window.__imwebAddToCartBadgeListener) return; // 셸 다중 임베드 표면 중복 등록 방지
		window.__imwebAddToCartBadgeListener = true;
		window.addEventListener('imweb:addToCart:added', function (e) {
			if (e.detail && e.detail.prodFound) return;
			var badges = document.querySelectorAll('._shop_cart_badge_cnt_wrap');
			for (var i = 0; i < badges.length; i++) {
				var cnt = parseInt(badges[i].textContent, 10);
				badges[i].textContent = String((isNaN(cnt) ? 0 : cnt) + 1);
				badges[i].removeAttribute('disabled');
			}
		});
	})();
</script>

  // youtube fullpage video function
  const handleFullscreenChange = function() {
    const body = document.body;
    const fullscreenElement = document.fullscreenElement;
    const is_youtube = fullscreenElement?.getAttribute('src').indexOf('youtube') > 0;

    if (document.fullscreenElement && is_youtube) {
      // 전체화면일 때
      body.classList.add('video_fullpage');
    } else {
      // 전체화면이 아닐 때
      body.classList.remove('video_fullpage');
    }
  }

  // event fullpage
  document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', handleFullscreenChange );

		// 비주얼섹션 배경 동영상 및 동영상 위젯 자동재생 환경 설정
	var section_youtube_list = [];

	var vimeo_player = [];

	var video_autoplay_youtube_list = [];
		$(function(){
		/* Bootstrap Sanitizer Custom */
		var customTooltipAllowList = $.fn.tooltip.Constructor.DEFAULTS.whiteList;
		customTooltipAllowList.table = [];
		customTooltipAllowList.thead = [];
		customTooltipAllowList.tbody = [];
		customTooltipAllowList.tr = [];
		customTooltipAllowList.td = ["rowspan", "colspan"];
		customTooltipAllowList.th = [];
		customTooltipAllowList.caption = [];
		customTooltipAllowList["*"].push("style");
		/* End Bootstrap Sanitizer Custom */
		$('body').smoothScroll({
			delegateSelector : 'a',
			speed : 1200,
			easing : 'easeInOutExpo'
		});
		// 커스텀위젯(V2)은 declarative shadow DOM 으로 렌더되어 내부 <a> 클릭 시 event.target 이
		// 호스트(.js-custom-widget-container)로 리타게팅됨 → 위 body 위임 smoothScroll 이 매치하지 못해
		// 네이티브 점프로 동작. composedPath 로 실제 앵커를 찾아 동일 조건일 때 동일 효과로 스크롤. (DEMO-1161)
		// 해시 대상 섹션이 존재할 때만 기존 smoothScroll 과 동일 파라미터로 스크롤. 성공 여부 반환.
		function smoothScrollToSection(hash, link){
			if(!$.smoothScroll || !hash || hash.charAt(0) !== '#') return false;
			var escapedHash = hash.replace(/(:|\.|\/)/g, '\\$1');
			if(!$(escapedHash).length) return false;
			$.smoothScroll({
				scrollTarget : escapedHash,
				speed : 1200,
				easing : 'easeInOutExpo',
				link : link || null
			});
			return true;
		}
		document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
			if(e.defaultPrevented || !e.composedPath || !$.smoothScroll || !e.target.closest || !e.target.closest('.js-custom-widget-container')) return;
			var path = e.composedPath();
			var anchor = null;
			for(var i = 0; i < path.length; i++){
				if(path[i].tagName === 'A'){ anchor = path[i]; break; }
			}
			if(!anchor || !anchor.hash) return;
			// 명시적 target(_blank/프레임명)은 작성자 의도로 보고 개입하지 않는다 — 새 탭 존중
			if(anchor.target && anchor.target !== '_self') return;
			var filterPath = $.smoothScroll.filterPath;
			var sameHost = !anchor.hostname || anchor.hostname === location.hostname;
			var samePath = filterPath(anchor.pathname) === filterPath(location.pathname);
			if(sameHost && samePath && smoothScrollToSection(anchor.hash, anchor)){
				e.preventDefault();
			}
		});
		// closed shadow root 대비 수신 계약: composedPath 는 closed 경계에서 절단되어 위 클릭
		// 핸들러가 앵커를 찾지 못한다. closed 트리 내부 코드(위젯 런타임 등 root 참조 보유자)가
		// 같은 문서의 섹션 해시를 detail.hash 로 담아 아래 composed 이벤트를 발행하면
		// light DOM 에서 동일 효과로 스크롤한다. 발행 측이 앵커 판별(같은 호스트/경로)을 책임진다.
		//   dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('imweb:smooth-scroll', {bubbles:true, composed:true, detail:{hash:'#...'}}))
		document.addEventListener('imweb:smooth-scroll', function(e){
			if(e.detail && e.detail.hash){
				smoothScrollToSection(e.detail.hash);
			}
		});
				$('.pms_check').remove();
						var recentScrollUrl = IMWEB_SESSIONSTORAGE.get('RECENT_PROD_SCROLL_URL');
		if(recentScrollUrl && recentScrollUrl !== document.location.href){
			IMWEB_SESSIONSTORAGE.remove('RECENT_PROD_SCROLL');
			IMWEB_SESSIONSTORAGE.remove('RECENT_PROD_SCROLL_URL');
		}
			});
</script>

  ;(() => {
    if (![isSafari(), isIos()].some(Boolean)) return;

    const $menuLinks = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('._mobile_nav a'));
    $menuLinks.forEach($menuLink => {
      if (!$menuLink.hash) return;

      $menuLink.addEventListener('click', () => setCookie('menu_link_hash', $menuLink.hash, 1));
    });

    const hash = getCookie('menu_link_hash');
    if (!hash) return;

    window.addEventListener('load', () => {
      const $section = document.querySelector(hash);
      scrollWindowToElement($section).then(() => deleteCookie('menu_link_hash'));
    });
  })();

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    // 헤딩 섹션 찾기
    const headingWrapper = document.querySelector('.tp-hero-heading-wrapper');
    
    if (!headingWrapper) return;
    
    // Intersection Observer 설정
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
        entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                const container = entry.target;
                
                // 애니메이션 클래스 추가
                if (!container.classList.contains('tp-animate')) {
                    container.classList.add('tp-animate');
                    
                    // 디버깅을 위한 콘솔 로그
                    console.log('Animation triggered for TALKPLE section');
                    
                    // 한 번 실행 후 관찰 중지
                    observer.unobserve(container);
                }
            }
        });
    }, {
        threshold: 0.2,
        rootMargin: '0px 0px -50px 0px'
    });
    
    // 컨테이너 관찰 시작
    observer.observe(headingWrapper);
    
    // 페이지 로드 시 즉시 확인 (디버깅용)
    setTimeout(() => {
        const rect = headingWrapper.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (rect.top < window.innerHeight && rect.bottom > 0) {
            headingWrapper.classList.add('tp-animate');
            console.log('Animation triggered immediately');
        }
    }, 500);
});

$("#text_w20250808fd45daa6d5c2d").find("._table_responsive").addClass("table").wrap($("<div />").addClass("table-responsive"));

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    // 헤딩 섹션과 콘텐츠 섹션 찾기
    const headerSection = document.querySelector('.talkple-main-header-section');
    const contentSection = document.querySelector('.talkple-content-showcase');
    
    if (!headerSection || !contentSection) return;
    
    // Intersection Observer 설정
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
        entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                const container = entry.target;
                
                // 애니메이션 클래스 추가
                if (!container.classList.contains('talkple-animate')) {
                    container.classList.add('talkple-animate');
                    
                    // 디버깅을 위한 콘솔 로그
                    console.log('Animation triggered for:', container.className);
                    
                    // 한 번 실행 후 관찰 중지
                    observer.unobserve(container);
                }
            }
        });
    }, {
        threshold: 0.2,
        rootMargin: '0px 0px -100px 0px'
    });
    
    // 두 섹션 모두 관찰 시작
    observer.observe(headerSection);
    observer.observe(contentSection);
});

<script>
	ALARM_MENU.init();
		SITE_ANIMATION.init('Y', 'Y');
	
	
	
	$(function () {

		const gallery_id = 'img_lg';
		let img_gallery_light_box = false;
    const sectionList = document.querySelectorAll('.section_wrap');

    sectionList.forEach(section => {
      const sectionId = section.getAttribute('id');
      const $sectionLightBox = $('body #' + sectionId);

      $sectionLightBox.lightGallery({
        selector: '._image_widget_lightbox',
        thumbnail: false,
        animateThumb: false,
        swipeThreshold : 20,
        showThumbByDefault: false,
        mode: 'lg-fade',
        speed: 200,
        galleryId: gallery_id,
      });

      if(history.replaceState && history.pushState){
        const current_url = location.href.indexOf('#') === -1 ? location.href : location.href.substr(0, location.href.indexOf('#'));
        const back_url = document.referrer.indexOf('#') === -1 ? document.referrer : document.referrer.substr(0, document.referrer.indexOf('#'));
        let history_push_flag = true;
        // 라이트박스 hash 커스텀(IE 10 이상)
        $sectionLightBox.on('onBeforeOpen.lg', function(){
          history_push_flag = true;
        });
        $sectionLightBox.on('onAfterOpen.lg', function(){
          const current_url_lg_id = location.href.indexOf('#') === -1 ? location.href : location.href.substr(location.href.indexOf('#'), location.href.indexOf('&'));
          if(current_url_lg_id.indexOf(gallery_id) > 0){
            img_gallery_light_box = true;
          }
        })
        $sectionLightBox.on('onAfterSlide.lg', function(event, prevIndex, index){
          if(img_gallery_light_box){
            if(history_push_flag){
              history.replaceState(null, null, current_url);
              history_push_flag = false;
            }
            history.replaceState(null, null, current_url + '#lg=' + gallery_id + '&slide=' + index);		// 슬라이드 히스토리 교체
          }
        });
        let history_back_flag = true;
        $sectionLightBox.on('onBeforeClose.lg', function(e){
          if(img_gallery_light_box){
            history_back_flag = true;
            if(window.location.hash.indexOf('lg=' + gallery_id) !== -1){
              history.back();
              history_back_flag = false;
            }
          }
        });
        $sectionLightBox.on('onCloseAfter.lg', function(){
          if(img_gallery_light_box){
            if(history_back_flag || window.location.hash.indexOf('lg=') !== -1){
              history.back();
            }
            history_back_flag = true;
            img_gallery_light_box = false;
          }
        })
      }

    });

		$('[data-toggle="tooltip"]').tooltip();

		
			});

	$(document).ready(function() {
		$('body').removeClass('page_ready');
		$('._bookmark').on('click', function(e) {
			var bookmarkURL = window.location.href;
			var bookmarkTitle = document.title;
			var triggerDefault = false;

			if (window.sidebar && window.sidebar.addPanel) {
				// Firefox version < 23
				window.sidebar.addPanel(bookmarkTitle, bookmarkURL, '');
			} else if ((window.sidebar && (navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase().indexOf('firefox') > -1)) || (window.opera && window.print)) {
				// Firefox version >= 23 and Opera Hotlist
				var $this = $(this);
				$this.attr('href', bookmarkURL);
				$this.attr('title', bookmarkTitle);
				$this.attr('rel', 'sidebar');
				$this.off(e);
				triggerDefault = true;
			} else if (window.external && ('AddFavorite' in window.external)) {
				// IE Favorite
				window.external.AddFavorite(bookmarkURL, bookmarkTitle);
			} else {
				// WebKit - Safari/Chrome
				alert(LOCALIZE.설명_즐겨찾기등록키안내( (navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase().indexOf('mac') != -1 ? 'Cmd' : 'Ctrl') + '+D') );
			}

			return triggerDefault;
		});


		
		
		// CRM Squad 서비스 SDK
		const c = window.crmService;
		c.boot({
			unitCode: 'u2025031467d3c014e2a1e',
			siteCode: 'S20250314cf488d6eecf35',
			memberCode: "",
			cartCode: "",
			isRegularly: !!''
		})

		SITE.initTermsModeElements();
	});
</script>


<script>
	// TODO: mfe에서 처리 필요
	$.ajax({
		type: 'POST',
		data: { },
		url: ('/shop/load_change_password.cm'),
		dataType: 'json',
		cache: false,
		success: function (res) {
			if (res.code === '1') {
				SECURITY_PRIVACY.openChangePasswordNotice(res.time)
			} else if (res.code === '2') {
				SECURITY_PRIVACY.openChangePasswordNotice(res.time);
			}
		}
	});


		if(LOCAL_STORAGE.getLocalStorage('AUTO_LOGOUT_TIME')) LOCAL_STORAGE.deleteLocalStorage('AUTO_LOGOUT_TIME');
		if(LOCAL_STORAGE.getLocalStorage('IS_AUTO_LOGOUT')) LOCAL_STORAGE.deleteLocalStorage('IS_AUTO_LOGOUT');

	</script>


<script>
    if (document.cookie.indexOf('clear_site_auth_token=1') !== -1) {
        if (typeof window.sessionStorage !== 'undefined') {
            window.sessionStorage.removeItem('imweb_site_auth_token');
        }
        document.cookie = 'clear_site_auth_token=; expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 GMT; path=/';
    }
</script>
